## 10. Deferred minors

Every Minor of both critiques is applied (§ 11) except the items below, and so is every « [P4] » carry of the P3 final review and outcome except the last four rows (revision 3). Each line says why.

| Finding | Not applied in P4 | Why |
|---|---|---|
| SEC-M14 | The live-switch listing of future bookings covered by a `livemode = false` purchase, and the « Test » badge on those bookings in the pro planning | P4 never serves production (go-live is P6), and both belong with the P6 live switch; Task 31 writes them into the outcome's P6 section. |
| COV-M4 (c) | Prefilling the dialog with the guest cookie customer's address | Rejected: the cookie can be planted by a cross-site navigation (LED:438), so it never prefills an identity field (SEC-I4, Decision 7); security wins. |
| COV-M7 (part) | `redactSubject` driven by `CODE_LENGTH` (OUT:128 rest) | Task 16 runs before `CODE_LENGTH` exists (Task 17) and the code length is fixed since P2; recorded as a residual in the outcome. |
| COV-M15 (part) | `src/app/[slug]/not-found.tsx` with « Voir la page » → `/{slug}` | Next renders a segment's own `not-found` for a `notFound()` thrown by that segment's page, so an unknown pro would get a link to its own 404; the global « Page introuvable » stays. The `/mon-espace` shell half is applied. |
| FR:F9 (pro half) | Engine guards `G(not rdv_free, 'stale')` on the offer create, offer-tickets, convert and convert-on-move statements, and the `readFreeBlockers` predicate as a guard of `updateTicketType` when it switches to « Gratuit » | P4 takes the client half (Task 4: `offerUnchanged` refuses a sale on a free type inside the batch, in every scope, so no money ever reaches a free type through a race) and touches none of those pro statements. What is left is a pro racing two of their own tabs within milliseconds; an offer left en vente on a free type sells nothing after Task 4 (the Boutique, the offer page and `offerUnchanged` all exclude `rdv_free`), and tickets offered or converted onto it stay usable on its linked tickets-mode events only, the class F2 already accepted; no tenancy, identity or money property is involved. P5 owns `rdv_free` (RDV libre) and rewrites those statements: Task 31 writes it into the outcome's P5 section. |
| FR:R11, FR:R8 / FR:F6 indexes | `booking (created_at desc, id desc) where status = 'confirmed'`, `booking (professional_id, status)`, `ticket_purchase (offer_id)`; and, noticed by revision 3, `booking (purchase_id)` (the engine's fulfilment and release reads and `purchaseByReference` filter on it) | P4 adds no migration (§ 1); the P3 outcome already gives these to the P6 index migration (with the parked 0013 and the `ticket` index of FR:R3). The P4 reads otherwise ride existing indexes (`ticket_purchase_checkout_session_uq`, `booking_occurrence_status_idx`, `booking_customer_idx`, `ticket_by_purchase_idx`). Task 31 copies the list into the outcome's P6 section. |
| FR:SEC-1 residual (c) | A sending identity (Resend key or subdomain) for sign-in codes apart from client mail, a circuit breaker across pro accounts, a cross-instance pacer | Infrastructure and product decisions for the go-live (P6); P4 never serves production, and its client-initiated mails are bounded by the throttle keys of § 3.3 (Decision 74). Task 31 writes it into the outcome's P6 section. |
| FR:COV-2 (limit) | Syncing the booked occurrences past the 200 of the Google backfill from the nightly cron | The cron would need a « backfill pending » marker per pro (a migration P4 does not have) and shares its 300 s `maxDuration` with the sweeps (OUT:126); the rest syncs on its next change today. Task 31 writes it into the outcome's P6 section. |

## 11. Revision log

### Revision 2

Revision 2 applies the security critique (`SEC-*`, 1 Critical, 7 Important, 16 Minor) and the coverage critique (`COV-*`, 1 Critical, 13 Important, 16 Minor). Where they disagreed, the security finding won: COV-I2 item 3 and COV-M4 (c) asked to prefill the dialog from the guest cookie, SEC-I4 forbids it (no prefill, no lock, Decision 7); COV-I4 proposed two buy + book sentences, SEC-I3's single neutral sentence was kept (Decision 64); COV-I2 item 2 and SEC-I4 both asked for « Ce n'est pas moi », SEC-I4's masked note and cookie clearing with the same flags were kept. Task count 31 → 33 (Task 21a added, Task 25 split into 25a and 25b). Decisions 63 to 71 added; Decisions 3, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 12, 19 to 24, 26, 27, 28, 31, 32, 36 to 38, 41 to 44, 46, 48, 49, 51, 53 rewritten.

| § 3.9 step 13, § 3.10 (Tasks 9, 11) | `createCheckoutAndAttach` wraps its body: anything that throws after the engine call (the attach UPDATE, a release) closes the open session and releases the purchase best effort (`abandonPurchase`) and rethrows; `startBuyAndBookCore` does the same for a throw of the product-name read. `booking-actions.db.test.ts` gains 4 tests (attach UPDATE throws after the session exists: one `expire`, purchase and booking expired, seat back; the title read throws: no session, seat released; a release that throws once after a failed create is retried; a failing `expire` with an address in its message is logged scrubbed and the seat still goes), `shop-actions.db.test.ts` 1 | a throw after the hold (`seanceTitleOf`, `attachCheckoutSession`, `releasePurchase`) left the seat held until the hold lapsed and the same typed address then got `COPY.BUYBOOK_NOT_POSSIBLE`; a shop purchase left a pending purchase and an open session |
| § 3.9 `registerFreeCore` (Task 10) | the typed `already_booked` path reads `activeBookingIdOf` and `seatsLeftOf` through `Promise.all` (one round trip, not two in sequence); `booking-actions.db.test.ts` gains the missing `activeBookingIdOf` case for its `gt(holdExpiresAt, now)` branch (a pending booking whose hold is live counts, one whose hold lapsed or ends exactly now does not) and a source guard that pins `Promise.all` | the registered address waited for two reads in sequence while a fresh one waited for none, widening the Decision 64 timing gap; the hold branch of `activeBookingIdOf` had no test |
| § 8.4 S24, S33, S19 (Tasks 9, 11) | tests that could not fail now can: the refused-attachment case of `booking-actions.db.test.ts` also asserts the result has no `checkoutUrl` and no `purchaseId` key (S24); a new case asserts that a verified session that is not yet an Owner at this pro and types its OWN address skips the hold ceiling while another address does not (SEC-I6, S33); the gateway-failure cases of `booking-actions.db.test.ts` and `shop-actions.db.test.ts` throw a message that CONTAINS the typed address and a session id and assert the log line is exactly the scrubbed one | the two no-address log checks compared a fixed fake message with the address, so they could never fail; the verified non-Owner branch of the ceiling and the absence of the URL key in a refused attachment were untested |
| § 3.6 (Task 6) | the retry worst case of the Checkout create is modelled as `CREATE_OPTIONS.timeout + 60_000` (the SDK waits a Retry-After of up to 60 s; whole call 90 s), not as two tries plus a 5 s back-off; a Retry-After above 45 s makes the retry fail as a clean create error (hold released, `COPY.CHECKOUT_FAILED`); the `CREATE_OPTIONS` JSDoc and the `checkout.test.ts` case say so and read `MAX_RETRY_AFTER_WAIT` from the installed SDK. `payment-mode.test.ts`: `runtimeSpecifiers` also collects `import("x")` and `require("x")` (a unit case on a fixture file pins it), so the client-boundary guard cannot be bypassed by a lazy import | the old case assumed a 5 s back-off and said the retry always kept `expires_at` above 30 minutes, which a Retry-After of 46 to 60 s breaks; the client-boundary guard read only static `import` and `export ... from` |

| § 3.13, § 5 mock row (Task 14) | `src/app/api/mock-checkout/route.ts`: `mockCheckoutState` (GET) and `mockPay` (POST) run inside a try / catch that logs `console.error("[mock-checkout] " + logSafeMessage(err))` and answers 500 with the plain text `Error` and the same headers (`serverError()`); the headers' CSP becomes `default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; form-action 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'` (the `/api/c` pattern, `X-Frame-Options: DENY` kept); `mock-checkout.test.ts` pins the try / catch, the log and the CSP | a throw from either call reached Next.js, which logs the raw error, and a driver error can carry the SQL parameters (the purchase id); the page only needs its inline style and a form that posts to this origin, so nothing else may load or be posted elsewhere |
| § 3.11, § 8.4 S8 (Task 12) | `abandonCheckoutCore` step 3 also answers `not_found` when `ref.booking === null` (a shop purchase), whatever its status, before the throttle and before any Stripe call; `release-actions.db.test.ts` gets the case (a pending shop purchase with a Stripe session, with a simulated one and with none, and a paid one: not found, `closeForRelease` and `expire` never called, the purchase left as it was, no throttle key consumed) | `closeForRelease` could expire the shop Checkout session and `releasePurchase` end the purchase, then the action reported `already_ended`: a seat release must not close a shop checkout |

| § 3.11 (Task 12) | `purchaseByReference`: the booking read's `leftJoin(event, ...)` and `leftJoin(ticketType, ...)` also carry `eq(event.professionalId, professionalId)` / `eq(ticketType.professionalId, professionalId)`; `purchase-reads.db.test.ts` gets a hand-built séance of this pro on another pro's event, and an RDV of this pro on another pro's ticket type: the reference describes the booking but its title is empty, its location null, and nothing of the other pro's text appears in it | the foreign keys of `occurrence` are single-column: a row that crossed two pros (a bug, an import) would have put another pro's event title and location into a page that only holds a purchase id |
| § 3.12 rule 6 (Task 13) | `webhook-core.ts`: `completed` + `unpaid` applies the own-session check of rule 7 before `markProcessing` (shared `ownSession` helper: `gone` / `other` / `own`; same log line, no ids); `webhook-core.db.test.ts` pins it (a foreign session id naming the purchase in its metadata: `ignored`, `processing_at` stays null, one scrubbed log line; its own session and a purchase with no session attached yet still go to `processing`; the S31 log test lists the new line) | rule 6 marked a purchase as processing for ANY session whose metadata named it, which exempts it from the nightly stale expiry (`processing_at` set); rule 7 already refused a foreign session before a release |
| § 8.4 S12 / SEC-M2, S31 (Task 13 tests) | the single « second payment » test is split into `other payment` (same session, another payment intent) and `other session` (another session, the same payment intent), each with its own stored-ids assertions, the second with a control (the same session and intent again is a plain duplicate); every case that prints a warning runs through `logs(run, expected)` (console captured, the exact lines asserted: the orphan line of L1116, the amount-mismatch line, the ignore reasons), so the DB run prints nothing | one delivery changed both ids, so removing either the `otherPayment` or the `otherSession` half of the duplicate check left the test green; the run printed about 20 `[stripe]` lines |
| § 1 URLs and logs, § 2.2, § 8.4 S31 (the tests of Tasks 13, 14, 15; the plan text of Tasks 20, 25b, 31) | new `src/test/source-guards.ts` and `source-guards.test.ts`: `rawErrorLogs(source)` (every `console` call of `log`, `info`, `warn` or `error` whose arguments name an identifier bound to a caught error, `.stack`, `String(err)` or a template interpolation of one, outside `logSafeMessage(...)`; comments, string text and regular expressions are not read as code), `codeView`, `sourceFilesUnder`; `webhook-core.test.ts`, `mock-checkout.test.ts` and `cron-auth.test.ts` assert `rawErrorLogs(ROUTE)` is empty instead of matching `/,\s*err\s*\)/`; `visitor.test.ts` and `payment-mode.test.ts` import the one source walker (their two copies are gone); the plan text follows: the Global Constraints bullet « URLs and logs », Task 20 (`pro-context.test.ts` drops its own `consoleStatements` and `RAW_ERROR_ARGUMENT`, test counts 15 to 14), Task 25b (`page-wiring.test.ts`) and Task 31 check 9 (the helper over every `src` file P4 touched) | the old guard caught only a trailing argument spelled exactly `err`: `error`, `e`, `err.stack`, `String(err)`, `${err.message}` and a call with a second argument after it all passed |
| § 3.6 (Task 6) | the 90 s of the Checkout create is the worst case when each try stalls, not a hard bound: `timeout` is stripe-node's socket idle timeout (`req.setTimeout` in `node_modules/stripe/cjs/net/NodeHttpClient.js`), so a response that keeps trickling bytes keeps its try alive; the `CREATE_OPTIONS` JSDoc, the § 3.6 text (the type block and the `create` bullet) and the `checkout.test.ts` case say so, and the test pins the `req.setTimeout` line of the installed SDK | « bounded at 90 s » promised more than an idle timeout gives; if the SDK ever makes it a wall-clock cap, or stops using the socket timeout, the test points back at the model |

| § 11 Revision 3.3, `payment-mode.test.ts` | `runtimeSpecifiers` also collects a backtick specifier that has no placeholder (a lazy `import` or `require` written with a template literal); one fixture line pins it, and a computed one stays uncollected | a lazy import written with backticks passed the client-boundary guard |
| § 3.13 (Task 14) | the `mock-checkout.ts` comment on a purchase that vanishes between `mockPay`'s read and the engine's own read says what really happens: the engine has already logged `[stripe] orphan payment -/-` (no ids) and counted it in the admin orphan counter before `mockPay` answers not found; only an id missing at the first read leaves the counter alone | the comment said there was no orphan to count, which is true of an unknown id and false of that race |

ERROR_CLASS : "sr-only"}>{error}</p>`, rendered from the first render (empty and `sr-only`, so out of the layout, until a failure); `auth-ui-guards.test.ts` pins two such regions with `{error}` as only child and no `{error && (` | the Global Constraints UI rule asks every failure line to sit in an always-mounted `role="alert"` region: a live region inserted together with its text is not reliably announced, and the previous `role="alert"` on a conditionally mounted `<p>` had exactly that flaw |

The landing, `/aide` and `/mon-espace` are the only users of `SiteHeader` (`/demo` has its own header) | the pro's link read « Mon espace » while leading to the dashboard, so on `/mon-espace` (« Mon espace client ») one label named two places |

### Revision 3.8 (2026-10-24): G4 review fixes, Task 25a round 1

| Where | Change | Why |
|---|---|---|
| § 2.4, § 3.23 `Boutique`, `src/lib/public-anchors.ts`, `src/components/public/scroll-to-anchor.tsx`, `src/components/public/boutique.tsx`, § 7 Task 25a | `public-anchors.ts` gains the pure `scrollToHashedAnchor(anchor, hash, doc): boolean` (scrolls to `doc.getElementById(anchor)` when `hash === "#" + anchor`, else does nothing); the client leaf `ScrollToAnchor({ anchor })` calls it once in an effect on mount with `window.location.hash` and `document`, renders nothing; `Boutique` renders `<ScrollToAnchor anchor={BOUTIQUE_ANCHOR} />` as the first child of its section (so a page without offers has no anchor and no leaf). Tests: `public-anchors.test.ts` (the rule), `boutique-anchor.test.ts` (the wiring, a source guard). The stale « Until then the link lands on the top of the existing page » comment of `BOUTIQUE_ANCHOR` is corrected | `loading.tsx` makes the page stream. On a client navigation to `/{slug}#boutique` (every « Voir la boutique » of the espace and of `/mon-espace`) Next's `ScrollAndMaybeFocusHandler` runs when the loading skeleton commits, finds no `#boutique`, scrolls to the skeleton and clears the hash fragment, so nothing scrolls when the content arrives; on a fresh load the browser's fragment scroll can run before React reveals the streamed block. Reproduced in the browser on tickets-dev before the fix (`/altinha-paris#boutique`: `scrollY` 0, `#boutique` top 633, both by a fresh load and by `window.next.router.push` from the espace); after it, `scrollY` 609, `#boutique` top 24 (`scroll-mt-6`) on both paths. A plain URL and a week-navigation link (no hash) are not moved. No other task relies on the old behaviour: Task 25b replaces `page.tsx` and keeps `<Boutique />` as it is |

The page replaces its hand-written `lapsed` expression (hold time only) with it: `"expired"` still renders the expired block, `"cancelled"` renders no block and the form shows `COPY.ENDED_CANCELLED`. A cancelled booking on an expired purchase is covered with a hold end live, ended or null; an expired purchase with a booking that is not cancelled, or with a payment on its way, and a paid purchase with a cancelled booking stay null. One more assertion of the wiring case pins that `refEnded` says `cancelled` for a cancelled booking whatever the purchase status | round 1 closed only the few seconds before the `checkout.session.expired` webhook. Once the session expires the purchase moves pending -> expired (`releasePurchase`, `expireStalePurchases`) but the cancelled booking stays cancelled, so `seatGoneOf` returned null, the page rendered the expired block (« Votre place a été libérée. Vous pouvez réserver à nouveau. ») and, because `checkout !== null`, hid the `COPY.ENDED_CANCELLED` notice: the layout the design note and Revision 3.10 forbid. In live mode a client coming back from a session that the pro's cancel just expired lands after the webhook almost every time |

| § 2.4 `next.config.ts`, `profile-reads.ts`, `profile-guard.test.ts` and `page-metadata.ts` rows, § 3.20 read wrappers, § 5 common rules and the `/[slug]`, offer and book/pack rows, M `next.config.ts`, M `src/lib/analytics.ts` (`RESERVED_SEGMENTS` exported), M `src/lib/next-config.test.ts`, M `src/lib/public/profile-reads.ts`, C `src/lib/public/profile-reads.test.ts`, M `src/lib/public/profile-reads.db.test.ts`, M `src/server/public-queries.ts` (D `getPublicPageProfile`, D `publicPageExists`, C `getPublicPage`), M `src/app/[slug]/layout.tsx`, M `src/app/[slug]/page.tsx`, C `src/lib/public/page-metadata.ts`, C `src/lib/public/page-metadata.test.ts`, M `src/app/[slug]/offre/[offerId]/page.tsx`, M `src/lib/public/page-wiring.test.ts`, M `src/lib/public/profile-guard.test.ts` | The book and pack redirect sources take a slug only when the segment is not a `RESERVED_SEGMENTS` entry (a negative lookahead built from the set, whole segment, any case), and the test compiles each source with the options of Next's `buildCustomRoute` (`strict: true`, `sensitive: false`, `delimiter: "/"`) and scans `src/app` so that a new top-level route that is not reserved fails. The `[slug]` layout answers `notFound()` for a segment that fails `SLUG_PARAM_RE` before any read, then reads `getPublicPage(slug)`, the request-cached read that `generateMetadata` and the page share: `readPublicPage` selects the public columns and a `suspended` flag in one query without the suspension filter (and runs none for a non-slug, because Next resolves the page's metadata alongside the layout); the layout only checks existence (a suspended pro exists, so `merci` keeps working), the page and its metadata take a suspended pro for an unknown one, and a suspended pro's projection never leaves the read. `getPublicPageProfile` and `publicPageExists` lost their last importer and are deleted; `planningEmptyState` is still read by `/[slug]` and stays. The offer page's metadata is `offerMetadata`: « Page introuvable », noindex, no referrer for every offer that is not on sale, whether the page answers `notFound()` (unknown slug, unknown or another pro's offer) or renders « Cette offre n'est plus proposée » (an archived, paused or type-archived offer: G4b fix round 2, the brief's « unknown, archived or foreign offer » rule; an archived id and an unknown one now carry the same title) | `/api/book/x` answered a 308 to `/api` and `/dashboard/pack/x` one to `/dashboard` (and the test compiled the sources with path-to-regexp's defaults, not the router's, so it could not see a case-insensitive match); a bot probe such as `/.env` or `/wp-login.php` cost two professional queries (the layout's `publicPageExists` and the metadata's `getPublicPageProfile`) and every `/[slug]` render ran two; an unknown or foreign offer, which is a 404, was titled with the business name, and so was an archived one, so the title told an archived offer id from an unknown one |

### Revision 3.12 (2026-10-26): G4c consistency sweep after G4b

| Where | Change | Why |
|---|---|---|
| § 2.4 (`visibility.ts` row, new `checkout-return-view.ts` row), § 3.21 (export block, new module block, séance page assembly), § 3.23 `CheckoutReturn` row, C `src/lib/public/checkout-return-view.ts`, C `checkout-return-view.test.ts`, M `src/lib/public/visibility.ts` (`liveHoldEnd`), `visibility.test.ts`, M `src/components/public/checkout-return.tsx`, `pending-holds.tsx`, `checkout-return.test.ts`, M `src/app/[slug]/seance/[occurrenceId]/page.tsx`, M `src/app/[slug]/offre/[offerId]/page.tsx`, `page-wiring.test.ts` (item 1) | The payment return is derived in one pure, client-safe module. `paymentReturnOf` returns `{ booked, block, shownPurchaseId, ended }` for the séance page; `checkoutBlockOf` returns the props of the block (the offer page calls it for a shop purchase); `returnViewOf`, `returnTextOf`, `releaseOutcomeText` and the `CheckoutReturnProps` type move out of the component. One rule decides the block and the line above the form, `seatGoneOf` (a booking the pro cancelled leaves no block and the line says `COPY.ENDED_CANCELLED`); the page's `refEnded`, whose « expired » half could never be displayed, is deleted. The hold rule is written once, `liveHoldEnd` of `visibility.ts`, which `seatGoneOf`, the booked state of a reference and the « gardée jusqu'à » line all ask. The Owner corner: when the Owner has an active booking the block describes only the purchase of THAT booking, so her pending purchase wins over a stale `?p=` of her own for the same séance (read once more by the id of her own booking, only when the URL names another one), and a stale reference never sits beside « Vous êtes inscrit(e) ». `checkout-return-view.test.ts` runs every case with a pro in Paris and a pro in Los Angeles (one DST day each) and pins the live and the lapsed hold, a booking cancelled under a pending or an expired purchase, processing, paid, a shop purchase and the Owner cases; the page-wiring guards pin the single call, the single clock and the absence of a second hold rule on the page. | The page derived `returnView`, `booked`, the block status, `resumeUrl`, `holdUntilLabel`, the `holdRows` exclusion and `refEnded` inline, pinned only by regexes on its source, and wrote the hold rule a second time next to `seatGoneOf`. An Owner with a pending booking X who opened an old `?paiement=annule&p=Y` of her own saw « Votre place a été libérée » while X was pending, without « Reprendre le paiement ». |
| § 2.4 (new `release-status.tsx`, `release-line.ts` rows), § 3.2 `COPY.ENDED_EXPIRED`, § 3.23 `CheckoutReturn`, `PendingHolds` and séance page copy rows, C `src/components/public/release-status.tsx`, C `src/lib/public/release-line.ts`, C `release-line.test.ts`, M `src/lib/public/checkout-return-view.ts` (`returnHeadingOf`), `checkout-return-view.test.ts`, M `src/components/public/checkout-return.tsx`, `pending-holds.tsx`, `checkout-return.test.ts`, M `src/lib/public/messages.ts`, `messages.test.ts`, M `src/app/[slug]/seance/[occurrenceId]/page.tsx`, `page-wiring.test.ts` (item 2) | (a) The heading of the payment block follows its view: « Votre paiement » for the paid and processing views (a cancel URL of a paid purchase read « Paiement interrompu » above « Paiement reçu »), « Paiement interrompu » for the others. (b) `PendingHolds` empties its line before each release. (c) The booking form of the séance page is keyed on `${visitor.state}:${mode}:${refView?.purchaseId ?? ""}`, so a soft navigation to another `?paiement=annule&p=` (the « Reprendre ou libérer ma réservation » link) starts the form over and the old refusal goes. (d) The Owner's own release from the block of the séance: the block has no live region of its own any more; a release starts with `announceRelease("")` and ends with `announceRelease(releaseOutcomeText(outcome))`, read by `ReleaseStatus`, one sr-only `role="status"` region that the séance page renders once outside every condition (the store behind it is the pure `createLineStore`, cleared when the region mounts and when it goes); the focus goes to `#seance-title`, restored by the dialog while the block is mounted and by the block itself when its answer comes back after the block went. (e) `COPY.ENDED_EXPIRED` becomes « Votre place n'est plus réservée. »: the data cannot tell a release the client chose from an expired hold (`releasePurchase` and the sweep both write `status` expired, `ended_by` system, no reason column), so one neutral line serves both. | The Server Action applies the revalidated page before its answer reaches the component, so after the Owner's own release the block, its dialog and its status line were gone: the focus fell to `<body>`, nothing was announced and the line read « a expiré » for something she had chosen. |

| § 2.1 `client-queries.ts` row, § 2.4 `profile-reads.ts` row, § 3.5 `resolveSpaceCustomer`, § 3.7 `resolvePublicPro` and `proOfPublicPage`, § 5 common rules and the espace row, M `src/lib/public/profile-reads.ts`, `profile-reads.test.ts`, M `src/lib/public/context.ts` (`proOfPublicPage`), `context.test.ts`, M `src/server/public-context.ts`, `public-context.test.ts`, M `src/server/client-queries.ts` (`getSpaceProfile` removed, `resolveSpaceCustomer` takes `PublicPro`'s names), M `src/app/[slug]/espace/layout.tsx`, `espace/page.tsx`, M `src/app/[slug]/page.tsx`, `merci/page.tsx`, `seance/[occurrenceId]/page.tsx` (`notFoundMetadata()`) | The G4b review left three things. `generateMetadata` of the merci, séance and offer pages reached the database through `resolvePublicPro` without the `SLUG_PARAM_RE` check that `readPublicPage` has, so `/.env/merci` and `/x.php/seance/y` still ran one professional query while the layout answered 404: `readPublicProfile` refuses such a segment (a segment of a valid shape, `/wp-admin/offre/z`, costs the layout's one query, which the page and the metadata now share). The séance, offer, merci and espace pages ran the layout's cached `getPublicPage` plus their own professional read: `resolvePublicPro` answers from the cached projection (a live pro: no second read), the espace layout and page read it directly, and only a suspended pro asked for by `merci` reads its row, once, because the projection never hands a suspended pro out; the suspension rule of each page is unchanged. The projection carries everything these pages use (id and user id as `professionalId` and `ownerUserId`, slug, business name, branding, zone, currency, window), so no page needed a column it lacks. `/[slug]`, `merci` and the séance page returned a bare or a hand-written not-found title: `notFoundMetadata()` | a bot probe or a page view should cost one professional query, not two; the metadata of a 404 should not differ page to page |
